Пункт 6a.4 архивирует именно тот файл → **эти строки останутся текстом без чекбокса, указывающим в архив**. Механическое действие при архивации: вернуть им `- [ ]` и снять шапку `ВЕДЁТСЯ В`. Это спасает ~15 из 53 без единого нового пункта в §5a. |

| docs/_TODO/SAAS_FOUNDATION/LOG.md | # Execution log — SAAS_FOUNDATION | 0 | 0 | 2026-08-17 | \| 2026-07-11 \| B4-roles-1 — GUC staff bypass → DB-role staff bypass (owner decision: separate DB roles, taskdb `#662`)           \| Owner-confirmed hardening: every patient-wall policy's staff bypass was `NULLIF(current_setting('app.actor', true), '') = 'staff'` — a plain session GUC settable by ANY session, including a patient session (provably spoofable by injection, not a real security boundary). Replaced it with a role-membership check via a new canonical helper `app.is_staff()` (schema `app`, `SECURITY INVOKER`, `STABLE`, `SET search_path = pg_catalog`): `SELECT pg_has_role(current_user, 'app_staff', 'MEMBER')` — `app_staff` is a single, fixed, canonical role name spelled out ONLY inside this function (not duplicated in any predicate). `rls-sql-renderer.mjs`'s `renderStaffActorCheck()` is the ONE chokepoint every staff-or-patient wrapper (`renderStaffOrPatientPredicate`, `..PatientChainPredicate`, `..ConditionalPatientPredicate`, `..ConditionalChainPatientPredicate`, `..PolymorphicPatientPredicate`, plus `p0-9-enforce-descriptors.mjs`'s fk_path enforce branch) calls through, so changing that one function flipped ALL 5 patient-ownership predicate shapes at once — verified by re-running `check-p0-8-{3,4,5}-policy-generator.mjs`/`check-p0-8-sql-renderer.mjs`/`check-p0-9-enforce-descriptors.mjs` (updated their hardcoded `app.actor`-substring assertions to `app.is_staff()`). Regenerated migration `0175_p0_8_b4_roles_1_is_staff_wall_rls.sql`: creates schema `app` + `GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA app TO PUBLIC` (found live — without it, ANY non-superuser NOBYPASSRLS role evaluating a patient-wall policy gets a hard `permission denied for schema app` ERROR, not a silent allow/deny) + `app.is_staff()` (`CREATE OR REPLACE`, idempotent) FIRST, then re-creates (`DROP POLICY IF EXISTS` + `CREATE POLICY`, same policy names as 0169-0174) the dormant policy for all **102 patient-owned SCOPED tables** (62 P0.8.3 public direct-org + 28 P0.8.4 public FK/denorm/polymorphic + 12 P0.8.5 integrator SCOPED — exactly the same 102 already patient-walled by 0169-0174; only the staff-bypass MECHANISM changed, no table gained/lost patient ownership) — byte-for-byte generator output of `p0-8-{3,4,5}-policy-targets.mjs --sql` filtered to `hasAnyPatientOwnership(descriptor)`. Registered in `meta/_journal.json` (idx 175). Added `deploy/postgres/p0-5b-role-split-staff-patient.sql` (extends B5/P0.5 — separate file, not touching the existing parameterized `p0-5-role-split.sql`): creates the two FIXED roles `app_staff`/`app_patient` (both `LOGIN NOBYPASSRLS`, no password/credential provisioned — dormant), asserts (fatal `SELECT 1/0` under `ON_ERROR_STOP`) both roles are LOGIN/non-superuser/non-createrole/NOBYPASSRLS, and — the actual security invariant — asserts `app_patient` has NO membership in `app_staff` (`REVOKE app_staff FROM app_patient` run unconditionally/defensively first, idempotent). No table grants (deliberately deferred to B5-v2, a separate task). Reversible (`-v p0_5b_down=1`), verified live. Added `docs/_TODO/SAAS_FOUNDATION/scripts/smoke-b4-roles-1-staff-role-boundary.mjs` (new, complements — does not replace — `smoke-r2-real-policy-isolation.mjs`) proving live on a scratch DB, reading the REAL committed `p0-5b-role-split-staff-patient.sql` + migration-0175 preamble (not re-typed copies): (a) a session authorized as `app_staff` → `app.is_staff()=true` → sees BOTH patients' rows org-wide, on two REAL rendered policies (direct-column AND a chain/EXISTS shape, via the actual renderer functions); (b) a session authorized as `app_patient` (not a member of `app_staff`) → `app.is_staff()=false` → with `app.patient_user_id` set, sees ONLY its own row, never the other patient's, on both shapes; (c) **the money proof** — still as `app_patient`, `SET ROLE app_staff` is REJECTED by Postgres itself (`permission denied to set role`) — proven via a real non-superuser `session_user` (established by `SET SESSION AUTHORIZATION app_patient`, which — unlike `SET ROLE` — changes `session_user` itself, so the subsequent `SET ROLE`'s permission check is genuinely against `app_patient`'s own memberships, not the harness's `postgres` superuser bootstrap); (d) empty context (no `app.org`/`app.patient_user_id`, still `app_patient`) denies both shapes, fail-closed. **Documented Postgres finding (not fudged, not asserted as a bug):** `SET SESSION AUTHORIZATION app_staff` attempted from the SAME already-`app_patient`-authorized session unexpectedly SUCCEEDS in this superuser-bootstrapped harness — Postgres's `SET SESSION AUTHORIZATION` permission check is against the connection's ORIGINALLY AUTHENTICATED role (fixed for the connection's life, here `postgres`, a real superuser — by design, so `RESET SESSION AUTHORIZATION` can always restore it), not the current `session_user`; a real production connection authenticated directly AS `app_patient` (not descended from a superuser) would not have this escape hatch. Proof (c) via `SET ROLE` is therefore the valid, harness-independent test and is the one this smoke asserts on. auto-dropped both times), NO deploy, app still connects as owner/BYPASSRLS today (P0.5/B5 not flipped), `app.org` GUC/app-layer routing untouched (B4-fanout, separate owner-gated stage). **NOT DONE (explicit, flagged to owner):** (1) table-level GRANTs for `app_staff`/`app_patient` — separate B5-v2 task by design. (2) wiring `app_staff`/`app_patient` into any real `DATABASE_URL` — B4-fanout. (3) `smoke-r2-real-policy-isolation.mjs` phase 5 ("simulate the flip" via `p0-9-enforce-descriptors.mjs`) is now STALE — it calls through the same `renderStaffActorCheck()`, so its simulated enforce predicates now also need `app.is_staff()`/an `app_staff` role, which that smoke's single-role GUC-toggle fixture never provisions; running it past phase 4 fails with `schema "app" does not exist`. NOT fixed here — doing so properly needs restructuring that smoke's one-role-toggles-`app.actor` design into a two-role design, out of proportion for this task; documented in a STALE-NOTE at the top of that file, superseded for the role-mechanism proof by the new dedicated smoke above. `smoke-r2` is not part of `check-saas-db-regression.mjs`'s automated gate. (4) **residual risk, explicitly flagged**: `app.patient_user_id` (and `app.integrator_user_id`) are STILL plain session GUCs — a session that has genuinely become `app_patient` could still `SET app.patient_user_id` to a VICTIM patient's id and read that victim's rows (the is_staff() fix closes the staff-impersonation hole, not the identity-impersonation one). Follow-up direction (not started): `GRANT SET ON PARAMETER app.patient_user_id`/`app.integrator_user_id` restricted so only a trusted DB-principal-context chokepoint can set them, or move patient identity onto the role/session the same way staff bypass just moved. Cluster-topology note: `app_staff`/`app_patient` are FIXED, cluster-global role names (Postgres roles are cluster-wide, not per-database) — fine for scratch/single-environment proof; if `dev`+`prod` ever share one Postgres cluster (as `host-psql-database-url.mdc` says they currently do), wiring these into real `DATABASE_URL`s (B4-fanout) will need per-environment namespacing or a single shared cluster boundary decision, not covered here.                                                                                                                                                  \| |
